════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  BESTIAIRE MONDIAL — 407 PROMPTS IMAGE POUR CHATGPT
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Mode d'emploi :
  1. Copier le prompt sous le numéro souhaité
  2. Coller dans ChatGPT (avec image generation activée)
  3. Télécharger l'image
  4. La nommer '<numéro>_<nom>.png' (ex: 001_Fenrir.png)
  5. La déposer dans _BookA/images/<XX_Tradition>/

In a small prophetic image in the corner: one man running away, without his companion, carrying a hatchet that seems to be struggling against him. The isba is surrounded by bones. Latin marginalia: "Likho — fatum malum" (evil fate) and one-eyed-misfortune terminology.   072. Vukodlak  (05_Slave)
  Image attendue : 05_Slave/072_Vukodlak.png
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Medieval bestiary illumination on aged parchment with gold leaf accents. A split composition. Left: night scene — a large wolf with distinctly human eyes runs through a Balkan village, his paws leaving prints in the moonlit mud. Through a window above: a man's empty bed, his body absent — he is the wolf. Right (same space, years later): the same man's grave at night, its earth disturbed. Rising from the grave: a figure both wolfish and dead — the Vukodlak in his posthumous vampire phase, retaining something of the wolf (slightly elongated teeth, faintly yellow eyes) but now in a dead-white human body. In the background of the right image: the village again, but a house with a black wreath — a family member recently died after the man's death. The connection: life as loup-garou becomes death as vampire. Latin marginalia: "Vukodlak — versipellis mortuus" (the dead skin-changer) and werewolf-to-vampire and Balkan-undead terminology. Left half moonlit silver running-wolf, right half grave-rising vampire-wolf, village background both images, house with black-wreath family-consumed, eyes connecting both forms.
